-- ========== دوال التحريك وسجلات النظام الخفيفة ==========
local function startContinuousProgressBar()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local t1 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)})
            t1:Play()
            t1.Completed:Wait()
            if not protectionActive then break end
            
            local t2 = tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(1.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)})
            t2:Play()
            t2.Completed:Wait()
        end
        tweens:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)}):Play()
    end)
end

local function startTerminalLoop()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local cyberOperations = {
                "> [FILTER] Zero-Lag explosion suppression active.",
                "> [ANTI-EXPLODE] Monitoring workspace efficiently...",
                "> [SHIELD] Concealing target instances instantly.",
                "> [OPTIMIZATION] Zero CPU overhead confirmed.",
                "> [LOCK] PropertyChangedSignal hooks active."
            }

            local randomPhrase = cyberOperations[math.random(1, #cyberOperations)]
            terminalBox.Text = randomPhrase .. "\n" .. terminalBox.Text
            
            local lines = string.split(terminalBox.Text, "\n")
            if #lines > 7 then
                table.remove(lines, #lines)
                terminalBox.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")
            end
            
            task.wait(1) 
        end
    end)
end

local function startGlitchLoop()
    task.spawn(function()
        while protectionActive do
            local randomHex = string.format("%08X", math.random(0, 4294967295))
            interceptLabel.Text = "EXPLOSIONS BLOCKED: [" .. blockedCount .. "] | HASH: 0x" .. randomHex
            task.wait(0.3)
        end
    end)
end

-- زر التشغيل والإيقاف
local function toggleProtectionWithEffect()
    if isProcessing then return end
    isProcessing = true

    if not protectionActive then
        protectionActive = true 

        protectBtn.Text = "⚙️ ACTIVATING FILTER..."
        
        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 4, 2)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 0)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(title, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 0)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(devsLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 0)}):Play()
        
        statusLabel.Text = "STATUS: ACTIVE | ZERO-LAG MODE"
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0)
        interceptLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 0)

        enableProtection()

        startContinuousProgressBar()
        startTerminalLoop()
        startGlitchLoop()

        task.wait(0.7)
        protectBtn.Text = "⏹️ DISABLE ANTI-EXPLODE"
        tweens:Create(protectBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 18, 5)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(btnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 0)}):Play()

    else
        protectionActive = false
        protectBtn.Text = "DECOMPILING..."
        terminalBox.Text = "> Purging active hooks...\n> Re-establishing standard state..."
        
        task.wait(0.8)
        disableProtection()

        tweens:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 8, 4)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(mainStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(100, 45, 15)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(title, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 0)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(devsLabel, TweenInfo.new(0.6), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 110, 70)}):Play()
        
        blockedCount = 0
        interceptLabel.Text = "EXPLOSIONS BLOCKED: [ 0x00 ]"
        interceptLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 130, 100)
        
        terminalBox.Text = "> Host online. Ready for zero-lag execution..."
        statusLabel.Text = "SYSTEM: STANDBY | VISIBILITY: NORMAL"
        statusLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 80, 80)
        
        protectBtn.Text = "💥 INITIATE ANTI-EXPLODE"
        tweens:Create(protectBtn, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 35, 10)}):Play()
        tweens:Create(btnStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {Color = Color3.fromRGB(140, 65, 20)}):Play()
    end

    isProcessing = false
end

local function troNotify(title, text, duration)
    pcall(function()
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
            Title = title or "TRO + WAVOR",
            Text = text or "",
            Duration = duration or 3,
        })
    end)
end

local function troRunSystem(system)
    if not system or not system.source then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        -- Compile/run this system as its own chunk so its locals cannot
        -- consume the main UI chunk's local-register budget.
        local compiler = loadstring
        if type(compiler) ~= "function" then
            troNotify("TRO + WAVOR", "loadstring غير متاح في هذا الـexecutor", 4)
            return
        end

        local okCompile, chunkOrErr = pcall(compiler, system.source, "@TRO_SYSTEM_" .. tostring(system.name))
        if not okCompile or type(chunkOrErr) ~= "function" then
            troNotify("❌ " .. system.name, "Compile Error: " .. tostring(chunkOrErr), 6)
            return
        end

        local okRun, runErr = pcall(chunkOrErr)
        if not okRun then
            troNotify("❌ " .. system.name, "Runtime Error: " .. tostring(runErr), 6)
            warn("[TRO + WAVOR] " .. system.name .. " -> " .. tostring(runErr))
            return
        end

        troNotify("✅ TRO + WAVOR", system.name .. " اشتغل", 2)
    end)
end

    closeBtn.TouchTap:Connect(function() closeAll() end)

    local divider = Instance.new("Frame")
    divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 55)
    divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, -36, 0, 2)
    divider.BackgroundColor3 = accentColor
    divider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
    divider.ZIndex = 103
    divider.Parent = page
    registerColorable(divider, "bg")

    local content = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    content.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 66)
    content.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, -78)
    content.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    content.BorderSizePixel = 0
    content.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    content.ScrollBarImageColor3 = accentColor
    content.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.5
    content.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    content.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    content.ZIndex = 102
    content.Active = true
    content.Parent = page

    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    layout.Parent = content

    local info = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    info.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 34)
    info.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    info.Text = "اضغط على النظام لتشغيل ملفه فقط • لا يوجد تشغيل تلقائي"
    info.TextColor3 = GRAY_TEXT
    info.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    info.TextSize = 10
    info.TextWrapped = true
    info.ZIndex = 103
    info.LayoutOrder = 0
    info.Parent = content

    for index, system in ipairs(TRO_SYSTEMS) do
        local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
        row.Name = "System_" .. tostring(index)
        row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 52)
        row.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE_BG
        row.BorderSizePixel = 0
        row.Text = ""
        row.AutoButtonColor = false
        row.Active = true
        row.Selectable = true
        row.LayoutOrder = index
        row.ZIndex = 103
        row.Parent = content

        local rowCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        rowCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
        rowCorner.Parent = row

        local rowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        rowStroke.Thickness = 1.5
        rowStroke.Color = accentColor
        rowStroke.Transparency = 0.45
        rowStroke.Parent = row
        registerColorable(rowStroke, "stroke")

        local icon = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 38, 0, 38)
        icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0.5, -19)
        icon.BackgroundColor3 = accentColor
        icon.Text = system.icon
        icon.TextColor3 = WHITE_TEXT
        icon.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        icon.TextSize = 18
        icon.ZIndex = 104
        icon.Parent = row
        registerColorable(icon, "bg")

        local iconCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        iconCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
        iconCorner.Parent = icon

        local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 7)
        nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -112, 0, 20)
        nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLabel.Text = system.name
        nameLabel.TextColor3 = BLACK_TEXT
        nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        nameLabel.TextSize = 12
        nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        nameLabel.ZIndex = 104
        nameLabel.Parent = row

        local descLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        descLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 27)
        descLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -112, 0, 17)
        descLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        descLabel.Text = system.title
        descLabel.TextColor3 = GRAY_TEXT
        descLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        descLabel.TextSize = 9
        descLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        descLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        descLabel.ZIndex = 104
        descLabel.Parent = row

        local runLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        runLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
        runLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -9, 0.5, 0)
        runLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 24)
        runLabel.BackgroundColor3 = accentColor
        runLabel.Text = "تشغيل"
        runLabel.TextColor3 = WHITE_TEXT
        runLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        runLabel.TextSize = 10
        runLabel.ZIndex = 104
        runLabel.Parent = row
        registerColorable(runLabel, "bg")

        local runCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        runCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        runCorner.Parent = runLabel

        local lastClick = 0
        local function run()
            local now = tick()
            if now - lastClick < 0.5 then return end
            lastClick = now
            troRunSystem(system)
        end

        row.Activated:Connect(run)
        row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(run)
        row.TouchTap:Connect(run)
        row.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(row, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundColor3 = LIGHT_HOVER
            }):Play()
        end)
        row.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(row, TweenInfo.new(0.15), {
                BackgroundColor3 = WHITE_BG
            }):Play()
        end)
    end

    return page
end

local systemsPanel = createSystemsPanel()

local function openSystems()
    local page = systemsPanel
    if openPanelRef == page then
        closeAll()
        return
    end
    if openPanelRef then
        closeAll()
        task.wait(0.3)
    end
    openPanelRef = page
    page.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
    page.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    page.Visible = true
    TweenService:Create(page, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, PANEL_W, 0, 500)
    }):Play()
end

createRailIcon(3, "🧩", "الأنظمة", function()
    openSystems()
end)
